<a id="qr"></a>
## QR & barcodes

Make and read QR codes, Wi-Fi QR, barcodes.

| Tool | What it does |
|---|---|
| [`barcode_generate`](#barcode_generate) | Barcode generator |
| [`qr_generate`](#qr_generate) | QR code generator |
| [`qr_read`](#qr_read) | QR code reader |
| [`qr_wifi`](#qr_wifi) | Wi-Fi QR code and card |

<a id="barcode_generate"></a>
### `barcode_generate` — Barcode generator

Make 1D barcodes as SVG or PNG: Code 128 (any printable ASCII; digit runs packed with subset C automatically), EAN-13 (12 digits, check digit added; or 13, check digit verified), UPC-A (11 or 12 digits, same) and Code 39 (A-Z, 0-9, space, - . $ / + %, optional mod-43 check). Same encoders, quiet zones, guard bars and human-readable digits as the website. Give "value" for one barcode, or "values" (up to 1000) to write one file per value into a folder; invalid values are reported, not fatal. PNG text uses the system monospace font. Web version: https://s3eidat.github.io/barcode-gen/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `value` | string | no | The value to encode (one barcode). |
| `values` | string[] | no | Or: many values; each becomes its own file in the output folder. |
| `symbology` | "code128" \| "ean13" \| "upca" \| "code39" | no (default `"code128"`) | Barcode type (default code128). |
| `code39Check` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Code 39 only: append the mod-43 check character. |
| `format` | "svg" \| "png" | no (default `"svg"`) | Output format (default svg). |
| `barWidth` | integer | no (default `2`) | Narrow bar (module) width in px, 1-6 (default 2). |
| `height` | integer | no (default `80`) | Bar height in px, 30-220 (default 80). |
| `showText` | boolean | no (default `true`) | Print the human-readable text/digits under the bars. |
| `foreground` | string | no (default `"#000000"`) | Bar colour, hex. |
| `background` | string | no (default `"#ffffff"`) | Background colour, hex. |
| `scale` | integer | no (default `2`) | PNG only: pixel scale (1×, 2× default, 4×…). |
| `output` | string | no | Where to write the result: a file path, or a folder ending in "/". Default: the server output folder (a temp folder unless --out-dir is set). |
| `returnBase64` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also return the output as base64 in the result (only for small files). Default false. |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | The written file (single value). |
| `bytes` | number |  |
| `mimeType` | string |  |
| `base64` | string |  |
| `text` | string | Encoded text (with check digit / check character). |
| `note` | string | e.g. "Check digit 1 added". |
| `width` | number | SVG width in px (PNG is width × scale). |
| `height` | number |  |
| `modules` | number | Barcode width in modules (without quiet zones). |
| `folder` | string | Batch: folder with one file per value. |
| `files` | object[] |  |
| `errors` | object[] |  |

**Example**

```json
{
  "value": "400638133393",
  "symbology": "ean13"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "text": "4006381333931",
  "note": "Check digit 1 added"
}
```

**Example** — Code 128 SVG

```json
{
  "value": "S3EIDAT-2026"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/S3EIDAT-2026.svg",
  "bytes": 2214,
  "mimeType": "image/svg+xml",
  "text": "S3EIDAT-2026",
  "width": 352,
  "height": 123,
  "modules": 156
}
```

**Example** — Two files, one error (12345 is too short).

```json
{
  "values": [
    "590123412345",
    "400638133393",
    "12345"
  ],
  "symbology": "ean13",
  "format": "png"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "folder": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/barcodes-ean13/",
  "files": [
    {
      "value": "590123412345",
      "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/barcodes-ean13/5901234123457.png",
      "text": "5901234123457"
    },
    {
      "value": "400638133393",
      "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/barcodes-ean13/4006381333931.png",
      "text": "4006381333931"
    }
  ],
  "errors": [
    {
      "line": 3,
      "value": "12345",
      "error": "EAN-13 needs 12 or 13 digits (you have 5)"
    }
  ]
}
```

<a id="qr_generate"></a>
### `qr_generate` — QR code generator

Make a QR code as SVG or PNG for a link, plain text, Wi-Fi login, contact card (vCard 3.0), email, SMS, phone call or map location. Same encoder as the website (qrcode.js: versions 1-40, error correction L/M/Q/H, smallest version that fits, best mask), UTF-8 so Arabic/emoji work. Options: size in px, quiet-zone margin, colours, square/rounded/dots modules, and an optional centre logo (forces level H). Returns the file path, the exact encoded text, version, module count and contrast warnings. Max about 2.9 KB of data at level L. Web version: https://s3eidat.github.io/qr-studio/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `type` | "url" \| "text" \| "wifi" \| "vcard" \| "email" \| "sms" \| "tel" \| "geo" | no | What the code holds. Default: "url" if url is given, else "text". |
| `text` | string | no | text: any text (encoded exactly as given). |
| `url` | string | no | url: web address; "https://" is added when no scheme is given. |
| `ssid` | string | no | wifi: network name. |
| `password` | string | no | wifi: password (ignored for open networks). |
| `security` | "WPA" \| "WEP" \| "nopass" | no | wifi: WPA (WPA/WPA2/WPA3, default), WEP, or nopass (open). |
| `hidden` | boolean | no | wifi: the network is hidden. |
| `firstName` | string | no | vcard: first name. |
| `lastName` | string | no | vcard: last name. |
| `phone` | string | no | vcard/sms/tel: phone number. |
| `email` | string | no | vcard: email address. |
| `company` | string | no | vcard: company. |
| `jobTitle` | string | no | vcard: job title. |
| `website` | string | no | vcard: website. |
| `address` | string | no | vcard: address. |
| `to` | string | no | email: recipient address. |
| `subject` | string | no | email: subject. |
| `body` | string | no | email: message body. |
| `message` | string | no | sms: message text. |
| `latitude` | number \| string | no | geo: latitude, -90..90. |
| `longitude` | number \| string | no | geo: longitude, -180..180. |
| `label` | string | no | geo: optional place label (?q=). |
| `ecc` | "L" \| "M" \| "Q" \| "H" | no (default `"M"`) | Error correction: L 7%, M 15% (default), Q 25%, H 30%. A logo forces H. |
| `mode` | "byte" \| "auto" \| "numeric" \| "alphanumeric" | no (default `"byte"`) | Encoding mode. "byte" (UTF-8, what the website uses), "auto" (numeric/alphanumeric when the text allows, smaller codes), or force one. |
| `version` | integer | no (default `0`) | QR version 1-40 (size), or 0 = smallest that fits (default). |
| `format` | "svg" \| "png" | no (default `"png"`) | Output format. |
| `size` | integer | no (default `1024`) | Image width/height in px (default 1024). |
| `margin` | integer | no (default `4`) | Quiet zone in modules (default 4; under 2 may not scan). |
| `foreground` | string | no (default `"#0d0f12"`) | Module colour, hex (default #0d0f12). |
| `background` | string | no (default `"#ffffff"`) | Background colour, hex (default #ffffff). |
| `shape` | "square" \| "rounded" \| "dots" | no (default `"square"`) | Module shape. Rounded/dots also round the three finder squares. |
| `logo` | string | no | Optional centre logo image (path or base64, max 5 MB). Covers ~22% of the code; error correction becomes H. |
| `output` | string | no | Where to write the result: a file path, or a folder ending in "/". Default: the server output folder (a temp folder unless --out-dir is set). |
| `returnBase64` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also return the output as base64 in the result (only for small files). Default false. |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | Absolute path of the written file. |
| `bytes` | number | Size of the written file in bytes. |
| `mimeType` | string |  |
| `base64` | string |  |
| `payload` | string | The exact text encoded in the QR code. |
| `version` | number |  |
| `modules` | number | Modules per side (version × 4 + 17). |
| `ecc` | string |  |
| `mode` | string |  |
| `mask` | number |  |
| `dataBytes` | number | Encoded length (UTF-8 bytes in byte mode). |
| `contrast` | number | Foreground/background contrast ratio (21 = black on white). |
| `warnings` | string[] |  |

**Example** — Encodes "https://example.com" as a 1024 px PNG.

```json
{
  "url": "example.com"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/qr-url.png",
  "bytes": 17533,
  "mimeType": "image/png",
  "payload": "https://example.com",
  "version": 2,
  "modules": 25,
  "ecc": "M",
  "mode": "Byte",
  "mask": 5,
  "dataBytes": 19,
  "contrast": 19.19,
  "warnings": []
}
```

**Example** — payload WIFI:T:WPA;S:Home;P:12345678;;

```json
{
  "type": "wifi",
  "ssid": "Home",
  "password": "12345678",
  "format": "svg"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/qr-wifi.svg",
  "bytes": 5788,
  "mimeType": "image/svg+xml",
  "payload": "WIFI:T:WPA;S:Home;P:12345678;;",
  "version": 3,
  "modules": 29,
  "ecc": "M",
  "mode": "Byte",
  "mask": 1,
  "dataBytes": 30,
  "contrast": 19.19,
  "warnings": []
}
```

**Example**

```json
{
  "type": "vcard",
  "firstName": "Sara",
  "lastName": "Haddad",
  "phone": "+962 7 9000 0000",
  "shape": "rounded"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/qr-vcard.png",
  "bytes": 138314,
  "mimeType": "image/png",
  "payload": "BEGIN:VCARD\r\nVERSION:3.0\r\nN:Haddad;Sara;;;\r\nFN:Sara Haddad\r\nTEL;TYPE=CELL:+962 7 9000 0000\r\nEND:VCARD",
  "version": 6,
  "modules": 41,
  "ecc": "M",
  "mode": "Byte",
  "mask": 4,
  "dataBytes": 101,
  "contrast": 19.19,
  "warnings": []
}
```

<a id="qr_read"></a>
### `qr_read` — QR code reader

Read (decode) a QR code from an image file: photo, screenshot or scan (PNG, JPEG, WebP, GIF, TIFF, AVIF, SVG). Uses jsQR like the website, trying several sizes and inverted (light-on-dark) codes. Returns the text plus what it is (Link, Wi-Fi, Contact, Email, SMS, Phone, Location, Event or Text) with parsed fields, and safety notes for links (http, hidden @ user part, punycode lookalikes, bare IPs, odd ports). Links are never opened. Reads one code per image. Web version (with camera): https://s3eidat.github.io/qr-scanner/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | string | yes | The image containing a QR code: a local file path (absolute, or relative to the server's --root) or base64 data (raw or a data: URI). |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `text` | string | The decoded content. |
| `type` | string | Link, Wi-Fi, Contact, Email, SMS, Phone, Location, Event or Text. |
| `fields` | object[] | Parsed fields (e.g. Network, Password). |
| `url` | object | For links: the real host and safety warnings. |
| `version` | number | QR version (1-40). |
| `bytes` | number | Number of data bytes. |
| `location` | object | Corner and finder positions in image pixels. |

**Example** — e.g. { text: "WIFI:T:WPA;S:Home;P:secret;;", type: "Wi-Fi", fields: [{label:"Network",value:"Home"},…] }

```json
{
  "image": "/path/to/photo-of-qr.jpg"
}
```

<a id="qr_wifi"></a>
### `qr_wifi` — Wi-Fi QR code and card

Make a Wi-Fi QR code that phones join by scanning (WPA/WPA2/WPA3, WEP or open; hidden networks), as PNG or SVG, or a printable PDF card like the website's: coloured header with the Wi-Fi symbol, "Scan to join", the QR code, network name and (optionally) the password, on an A6 card, an A4 poster, or A4 sheets of 4 or 9 cards to cut. Special characters (; , : " \) are escaped. The PDF card uses English labels and Latin text only for now. Web version: https://s3eidat.github.io/wifi-card/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ssid` | string | yes | Network name (SSID). |
| `password` | string | no (default `""`) | Wi-Fi password (ignored for open networks). |
| `security` | "WPA" \| "WEP" \| "nopass" | no (default `"WPA"`) | WPA (WPA/WPA2/WPA3, default), WEP, or nopass (open network). |
| `hidden` | boolean | no (default `false`) | The network is hidden (not broadcast). |
| `format` | "png" \| "svg" \| "pdf" | no (default `"pdf"`) | pdf = printable card sheet (default); png/svg = just the QR code. |
| `layout` | "a6" \| "a4" \| "a4x4" \| "a4x9" | no (default `"a4x4"`) | PDF: a6 = one A6 card; a4 = one A4 poster; a4x4 = A4 sheet of 4 cards (default); a4x9 = A4 sheet of 9 small cards. |
| `theme` | "teal" \| "ink" \| "coral" \| "violet" \| "saver" | no (default `"teal"`) | PDF: card colour; "saver" is an ink-saving outline. |
| `title` | string | no (default `""`) | PDF: heading on the card (default "Wi-Fi"). |
| `showPassword` | boolean | no (default `true`) | PDF: print the password under the code. |
| `size` | integer | no (default `1024`) | PNG/SVG: image size in px. |
| `margin` | integer | no (default `4`) | PNG/SVG: quiet zone in modules. |
| `output` | string | no | Where to write the result: a file path, or a folder ending in "/". Default: the server output folder (a temp folder unless --out-dir is set). |
| `returnBase64` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also return the output as base64 in the result (only for small files). Default false. |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string |  |
| `bytes` | number |  |
| `mimeType` | string |  |
| `base64` | string |  |
| `pages` | number |  |
| `payload` | string | The exact text in the QR code. |
| `version` | number |  |
| `modules` | number |  |
| `warnings` | string[] |  |

**Example** — A4 sheet with 4 cards; payload WIFI:T:WPA;S:S3eidat Café;P:coffee-is-free;;

```json
{
  "ssid": "S3eidat Café",
  "password": "coffee-is-free"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/wifi-card.pdf",
  "bytes": 6360,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 1,
  "payload": "WIFI:T:WPA;S:S3eidat Café;P:coffee-is-free;;",
  "version": 4,
  "modules": 33,
  "warnings": []
}
```

**Example** — payload WIFI:T:nopass;S:Guest;;

```json
{
  "ssid": "Guest",
  "security": "nopass",
  "format": "png"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/wifi-qr.png",
  "bytes": 17598,
  "mimeType": "image/png",
  "payload": "WIFI:T:nopass;S:Guest;;",
  "version": 2,
  "modules": 25,
  "warnings": []
}
```

<a id="docs"></a>
## Document makers

Invoices, receipts, business cards, labels, calendars and CVs as PDF.

| Tool | What it does |
|---|---|
| [`doc_attendance_sheet`](#doc_attendance_sheet) | Attendance sheet PDF |
| [`doc_business_cards`](#doc_business_cards) | Business cards PDF |
| [`doc_calendar`](#doc_calendar) | Calendar PDF |
| [`doc_invoice`](#doc_invoice) | Invoice PDF |
| [`doc_labels`](#doc_labels) | Label sheets PDF |
| [`doc_receipt`](#doc_receipt) | Receipt PDF |
| [`doc_resume`](#doc_resume) | Resume / CV PDF |

<a id="doc_attendance_sheet"></a>
### `doc_attendance_sheet` — Attendance sheet PDF

Make a printable attendance / sign-in sheet PDF: names down the side, one column per date for a month (skipping chosen weekdays, e.g. Fri+Sat or Sat+Sun), a run of N sessions from a start date, or N blank columns; tick boxes or wide signature cells; row numbers, a total column, extra empty rows, title, group and teacher. Same page maths as the website: rows that don't fit continue on more pages, and too many date columns are split across pages with the same names. Also returns a blank register CSV. English labels, Latin text only for now. Web version: https://s3eidat.github.io/attendance-sheet/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `names` | string \| string[] | yes | Names, one per line (leading "1." numbering and anything after a tab are removed), or a list. |
| `title` | string | no (default `""`) | Title (default "Attendance"). |
| `group` | string | no (default `""`) | Class / group, shown under the title. |
| `teacher` | string | no (default `""`) | Teacher, shown under the title. |
| `mode` | "month" \| "sessions" \| "blank" | no (default `"month"`) | month = every day of a month; sessions = N dates from a start date; blank = N unnamed columns. |
| `month` | string | no | month mode: YYYY-MM (default this month). |
| `start` | string | no | sessions mode: first date YYYY-MM-DD (default today). |
| `count` | integer | no (default `12`) | sessions/blank: number of columns. |
| `skip` | integer[] | no (default `[0,6]`) | Weekdays to skip, 0 = Sunday … 6 = Saturday (default [0, 6] = weekends Sat+Sun; use [5, 6] for Fri+Sat). |
| `cell` | "box" \| "sign" | no (default `"box"`) | box = tick boxes, sign = wide cells for signatures. |
| `paper` | "A4" \| "Letter" | no (default `"A4"`) |  |
| `orientation` | "landscape" \| "portrait" | no (default `"landscape"`) |  |
| `rowHeight` | number | no | Row height in mm (default 8, or 13 for signature cells). |
| `extraRows` | integer | no (default `2`) | Empty rows after the names. |
| `numbers` | boolean | no (default `true`) | Row number column. |
| `total` | boolean | no (default `true`) | Total column. |
| `output` | string | no | Where to write the result: a file path, or a folder ending in "/". Default: the server output folder (a temp folder unless --out-dir is set). |
| `returnBase64` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also return the output as base64 in the result (only for small files). Default false. |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | Absolute path of the written PDF. |
| `bytes` | number | Size in bytes. |
| `pages` | number | Number of pages. |
| `mimeType` | string |  |
| `base64` | string |  |
| `names` | number |  |
| `columns` | number |  |
| `rowsPerPage` | number |  |
| `columnChunks` | number | Pages the date columns are split over. |
| `dates` | string[] | Column keys (YYYY-MM-DD, or c1… in blank mode). |
| `csv` | string | A blank register as CSV (Name + one column per date). |

**Example** — 22 date columns, 1 page.

```json
{
  "names": "Ahmad Khalil\nLina Haddad\nOmar Saleh",
  "group": "Grade 7 B · Science",
  "teacher": "Ms. Rana",
  "month": "2026-11",
  "skip": [
    5,
    6
  ]
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/attendance.pdf",
  "bytes": 11902,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 1,
  "names": 3,
  "columns": 22,
  "rowsPerPage": 19,
  "columnChunks": 1,
  "dates": [
    "2026-11-01",
    "2026-11-02",
    "2026-11-03",
    "2026-11-04",
    "2026-11-05",
    "2026-11-08",
    "2026-11-09",
    "2026-11-10",
    "2026-11-11",
    "2026-11-12",
    "2026-11-15",
    "2026-11-16",
    "2026-11-17",
    "2026-11-18",
    "2026-11-19",
    "2026-11-22",
    "2026-11-23",
    "2026-11-24",
    "2026-11-25",
    "2026-11-26",
    "2026-11-29",
    "2026-11-30"
  ],
  "csv": "Name,2026-11-01,2026-11-02,2026-11-03,2026-11-04,2026-11-05,2026-11-08,2026-11-09,2026-11-10,2026-11-11,2026-11-12,2026-11-15,2026-11-16,2026-11-17,2026-11-18,2026-11-19,2026-11-22,2026-11-23,2026-11-24,2026-11-25,2026-11-26,2026-11-29,2026-11-30\r\nAhmad Khalil,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,\r\nLina Haddad,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,\r\nOmar Saleh,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,\r\n"
}
```

<a id="doc_business_cards"></a>
### `doc_business_cards` — Business cards PDF

Design business cards and get a print-ready PDF sheet: cards centred on A4 or Letter with crop marks (10 per sheet for 85 × 55 mm EU or 3.5 × 2 in US cards), plus a back side page mirrored for long-edge duplex printing. Four front templates (classic, band, centered, minimal), backs with your logo, a "scan to save my contact" vCard QR code, or blank; six colour themes or your own accent/paper/ink colours; optional logo and a vCard QR on the front. Also returns the vCard text. Print at 100% / actual size. Latin text only for now (the website also does an Arabic side). Web version: https://s3eidat.github.io/business-card/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | Your name. |
| `title` | string | no (default `""`) | Job title. |
| `company` | string | no (default `""`) | Company. |
| `phone` | string | no (default `""`) |  |
| `email` | string | no (default `""`) |  |
| `website` | string | no (default `""`) |  |
| `address` | string | no (default `""`) |  |
| `tagline` | string | no (default `""`) | Short line for the back. |
| `template` | "classic" \| "band" \| "centered" \| "minimal" | no (default `"classic"`) | Front layout. |
| `back` | "logo" \| "qr" \| "none" | no (default `"logo"`) | Back side: logo/company on the accent colour, a contact QR code, or none. |
| `qrFront` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also put the contact QR code on the front. |
| `theme` | "teal" \| "navy" \| "charcoal" \| "forest" \| "violet" \| "mono" | no (default `"teal"`) | Colour preset (navy = navy & gold). |
| `accent` | string | no | Override the accent colour. |
| `paperColor` | string | no | Override the card background colour. |
| `ink` | string | no | Override the text colour. |
| `size` | "eu" \| "us" | no (default `"eu"`) | eu = 85 × 55 mm, us = 3.5 × 2 in. |
| `sheet` | "A4" \| "Letter" | no (default `"A4"`) | Paper for the print sheet. |
| `gap` | number | no (default `0`) | Gap between cards in mm (0 = shared cut lines). |
| `sides` | "both" \| "front" \| "back" | no (default `"both"`) | Which pages to include (both = front page then mirrored back page). |
| `logo` | string | no | Optional logo image: a PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF/SVG file path or base64. Drawn as-is (keep it small). |
| `output` | string | no | Where to write the result: a file path, or a folder ending in "/". Default: the server output folder (a temp folder unless --out-dir is set). |
| `returnBase64` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also return the output as base64 in the result (only for small files). Default false. |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | Absolute path of the written PDF. |
| `bytes` | number | Size in bytes. |
| `pages` | number | Number of pages. |
| `mimeType` | string |  |
| `base64` | string |  |
| `cardsPerSheet` | number |  |
| `columns` | number |  |
| `rows` | number |  |
| `vcard` | string | The vCard 3.0 text (what the QR code holds). |
| `qrVersion` | number \| null |  |

**Example** — A4 sheet of 10 fronts + mirrored sheet of 10 QR backs.

```json
{
  "name": "Lina Haddad",
  "title": "Optometrist",
  "company": "Lumen Eye Clinic",
  "phone": "+962 79 000 0000",
  "email": "lina@lumen.example",
  "website": "lumen.example",
  "address": "Amman, Jordan",
  "back": "qr"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/lina-haddad-cards.pdf",
  "bytes": 18458,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 2,
  "cardsPerSheet": 10,
  "columns": 2,
  "rows": 5,
  "vcard": "BEGIN:VCARD\r\nVERSION:3.0\r\nN:Haddad;Lina;;;\r\nFN:Lina Haddad\r\nORG:Lumen Eye Clinic\r\nTITLE:Optometrist\r\nTEL;TYPE=CELL:+962 79 000 0000\r\nEMAIL;TYPE=INTERNET:lina@lumen.example\r\nURL:lumen.example\r\nADR;TYPE=WORK:;;Amman\\, Jordan;;;;\r\nEND:VCARD",
  "qrVersion": 11
}
```

<a id="doc_calendar"></a>
### `doc_calendar` — Calendar PDF

Make a printable calendar PDF: a month grid (one page per month, several months in a row if you like) or a whole year on one page, A4 or Letter, landscape or portrait. Week starts on Saturday (default, like the website), Sunday or Monday; optional Hijri (Umm al-Qura) dates; events (one-off or yearly, 7 colours) shown in the day cells, or as coloured days plus a legend in the year view; optional title. English names by default; other Latin-script locales (fr, de, es, tr…) use their own month/day names. Web version: https://s3eidat.github.io/calendar-maker/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `view` | "month" \| "year" | no (default `"month"`) | month = month grid pages, year = 12 months on one page. |
| `year` | integer | no | Year (default: this year). |
| `month` | integer | no | Month 1-12 (month view; default: this month). |
| `months` | integer | no (default `1`) | Month view: how many consecutive months (one page each). |
| `weekStart` | 0 \| 1 \| 6 | no (default `6`) | First day of the week: 6 = Saturday (default), 0 = Sunday, 1 = Monday. |
| `hijri` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Show Hijri (Umm al-Qura) dates. |
| `paper` | "A4" \| "Letter" | no (default `"A4"`) |  |
| `orientation` | "landscape" \| "portrait" | no (default `"landscape"`) |  |
| `title` | string | no (default `""`) | Optional title above the month name, e.g. "Family calendar". |
| `locale` | string | no (default `"en"`) | Language for month/day names (default en). Latin-script locales only. |
| `events` | object[] | no (default `[]`) | Events to show. |
| `output` | string | no | Where to write the result: a file path, or a folder ending in "/". Default: the server output folder (a temp folder unless --out-dir is set). |
| `returnBase64` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also return the output as base64 in the result (only for small files). Default false. |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | Absolute path of the written PDF. |
| `bytes` | number | Size in bytes. |
| `pages` | number | Number of pages. |
| `mimeType` | string |  |
| `base64` | string |  |
| `from` | string |  |
| `to` | string | Last month covered, YYYY-MM. |

**Example**

```json
{
  "year": 2026,
  "month": 12,
  "title": "Family calendar",
  "hijri": true,
  "events": [
    {
      "date": "2026-12-03",
      "text": "Sara's birthday",
      "color": "purple",
      "yearly": true
    }
  ]
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/calendar-2026-12.pdf",
  "bytes": 4028,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 1,
  "from": "2026-12",
  "to": "2026-12"
}
```

**Example**

```json
{
  "view": "year",
  "year": 2027,
  "weekStart": 1,
  "orientation": "portrait"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/calendar-2027.pdf",
  "bytes": 11458,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 1,
  "from": "2027-01",
  "to": "2027-12"
}
```

<a id="doc_invoice"></a>
### `doc_invoice` — Invoice PDF

Make a clean A4 invoice PDF: your business (logo, name, address, email, phone, tax ID), client, invoice number and dates, line items with quantity, unit price, per-line discount % and tax %, subtotal, discount, tax per rate and total, notes, payment terms, and an optional PAID stamp. Same maths as the website: every line is rounded to the currency's minor unit (2 decimals, 3 for JOD/KWD/BHD/OMR/TND…, 0 for JPY…) with half-up rounding; amounts formatted en-US style ($1,234.50, JOD 10.124). Long item lists continue on more pages. Returns the PDF path and the computed totals. Latin text only for now. Web version: https://s3eidat.github.io/invoice-maker/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `number` | string | no (default `"INV-0001"`) | Invoice number. |
| `date` | string | no | Issue date, YYYY-MM-DD (default today). |
| `due` | string | no | Due date, YYYY-MM-DD (optional). |
| `currency` | "USD" \| "EUR" \| "GBP" \| "JOD" \| "SAR" \| "AED" \| "EGP" \| "KWD" \| "QAR" \| "BHD" \| "OMR" \| "IQD" \| "LBP" \| "MAD" \| "TND" \| "DZD" \| "TRY" \| "INR" \| "PKR" \| "CAD" \| "AUD" \| "NZD" \| "JPY" \| "CNY" \| "CHF" \| "SEK" \| "NOK" \| "DKK" \| "PLN" \| "ZAR" \| "BRL" \| "MXN" \| "SGD" | no (default `"USD"`) | Currency code. |
| `accent` | string | no (default `"#0e9f9a"`) | Accent colour (hex). |
| `paid` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Mark as paid (shows "Amount paid" and a PAID stamp). |
| `from` | object | no (default `{"name":"","email":"","phone":"","address":"","taxId":""}`) | Your business: name, email, phone, address, taxId. |
| `to` | object | no (default `{"name":"","email":"","address":""}`) | The client (bill to): name, email, address. |
| `items` | object[] | yes | Line items: desc, qty, price, disc %, tax %. |
| `notes` | string | no (default `""`) | Notes shown at the bottom. |
| `terms` | string | no (default `""`) | Payment terms shown at the bottom. |
| `logo` | string | no | Optional logo image: a PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF/SVG file path or base64. Drawn as-is (keep it small). |
| `output` | string | no | Where to write the result: a file path, or a folder ending in "/". Default: the server output folder (a temp folder unless --out-dir is set). |
| `returnBase64` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also return the output as base64 in the result (only for small files). Default false. |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | Absolute path of the written PDF. |
| `bytes` | number | Size in bytes. |
| `pages` | number | Number of pages. |
| `mimeType` | string |  |
| `base64` | string |  |
| `subtotal` | number |  |
| `discount` | number |  |
| `tax` | number |  |
| `total` | number |  |
| `taxByRate` | object | Tax amount per rate (%). |
| `lineAmounts` | number[] | Net amount of each line (after discount, before tax). |
| `currency` | string |  |
| `decimals` | number |  |
| `formattedTotal` | string |  |

**Example** — Subtotal $1,540.00, discount $34.00, tax 8% $120.48, total $1,626.48.

```json
{
  "number": "INV-0042",
  "currency": "USD",
  "from": {
    "name": "Northwind Studio",
    "email": "hello@northwind.studio"
  },
  "to": {
    "name": "Blue Fern Café"
  },
  "items": [
    {
      "desc": "Brand identity design",
      "qty": 1,
      "price": 1200,
      "tax": 8
    },
    {
      "desc": "Menu layout (per page)",
      "qty": 4,
      "price": 85,
      "disc": 10,
      "tax": 8
    }
  ],
  "terms": "Bank transfer within 14 days."
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/invoice-INV-0042.pdf",
  "bytes": 3109,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 1,
  "subtotal": 1540,
  "discount": 34,
  "tax": 120.48,
  "total": 1626.48,
  "taxByRate": {
    "8": 120.48
  },
  "lineAmounts": [
    1200,
    306
  ],
  "currency": "USD",
  "decimals": 2,
  "formattedTotal": "$1,626.48"
}
```

<a id="doc_labels"></a>
### `doc_labels` — Label sheets PDF

Print address or product labels on standard sticker sheets as PDF: A4 21/24/14/40/65-up (L7160, L7159, L7163, L7654, L7651 sizes) or Letter 30/10/80-up (5160, 5163, 5167 sizes), or custom geometry in mm. Text modes: "list" (one label per block, blank line between labels), "csv" (rows filled into a template like "{Name}\n{Street}\n{City} {Postcode}", header row or {1},{2}…), or "same" (one text × count). Copies per label, start at a later position to reuse a part-used sheet, alignment, font, size, bold first line, border, shrink-to-fit and printer offset nudges. Print at 100% / actual size. Up to 3000 labels. Latin text only for now. Web version: https://s3eidat.github.io/label-maker/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | "list" \| "csv" \| "same" | no (default `"list"`) | list = blocks separated by a blank line; csv = rows + template; same = one text repeated. |
| `text` | string | no | The labels text (list blocks, CSV/TSV, or the single text). |
| `path` | string | no | Or: a text/CSV file path to read it from. |
| `template` | string | no (default `""`) | csv: template with {Column name} or {1}-style placeholders (empty = all columns, one per line). |
| `header` | boolean | no (default `true`) | csv: the first row holds column names. |
| `count` | integer | no (default `21`) | same: how many labels. |
| `copies` | integer | no (default `1`) | list/csv: copies of each label. |
| `preset` | "a4-21" \| "a4-24" \| "a4-14" \| "a4-40" \| "a4-65" \| "lt-30" \| "lt-10" \| "lt-80" \| "custom" | no (default `"a4-21"`) | Sheet: a4-21 (63.5×38.1), a4-24 (63.5×33.9), a4-14 (99.1×38.1), a4-40 (45.7×25.4), a4-65 (38.1×21.2), lt-30 (2⅝×1 in), lt-10 (4×2 in), lt-80 (1¾×½ in), or custom. |
| `custom` | object | no | preset "custom": paper, cols, rows, w, h, top, left, gx, gy (mm). |
| `startAt` | integer | no (default `1`) | Start at this label position on the first sheet (skips used labels). |
| `fontSize` | number | no (default `11`) | Font size in pt (default 11). |
| `font` | "sans" \| "serif" \| "mono" | no (default `"sans"`) |  |
| `align` | "left" \| "center" \| "right" | no (default `"center"`) |  |
| `valign` | "top" \| "middle" | no (default `"middle"`) |  |
| `bold` | boolean | no (default `true`) | Bold first line (e.g. the name). |
| `fit` | boolean | no (default `true`) | Shrink text (in 0.5 pt steps, min 5 pt) until it fits the label. |
| `border` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Draw a thin rounded border around each label. |
| `padding` | number | no (default `3`) | Inner padding in mm. |
| `offsetX` | number | no (default `0`) | Printer nudge right (mm, negative = left). |
| `offsetY` | number | no (default `0`) | Printer nudge down (mm, negative = up). |
| `output` | string | no | Where to write the result: a file path, or a folder ending in "/". Default: the server output folder (a temp folder unless --out-dir is set). |
| `returnBase64` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also return the output as base64 in the result (only for small files). Default false. |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | Absolute path of the written PDF. |
| `bytes` | number | Size in bytes. |
| `pages` | number | Number of pages. |
| `mimeType` | string |  |
| `base64` | string |  |
| `labels` | number | Labels printed. |
| `perSheet` | number |  |
| `shrunk` | number | Labels whose text was shrunk to fit. |
| `truncated` | number | Labels whose text still did not fit (cut at the bottom). |

**Example** — 2 address labels on an A4 21-up sheet.

```json
{
  "text": "Lina Haddad\n12 Rainbow Street\nAmman 11181\n\nOmar Saleh\n4 Garden Lane\nIrbid 21110",
  "border": true
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/labels.pdf",
  "bytes": 1545,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 1,
  "labels": 2,
  "perSheet": 21,
  "shrunk": 0,
  "truncated": 0
}
```

**Example**

```json
{
  "mode": "csv",
  "text": "Name,City\nLina,Amman\nOmar,Irbid",
  "template": "{Name}\n{City}",
  "preset": "lt-30"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/labels.pdf",
  "bytes": 1091,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 1,
  "labels": 2,
  "perSheet": 30,
  "shrunk": 0,
  "truncated": 0
}
```

**Example**

```json
{
  "mode": "same",
  "text": "Homemade Fig Jam\n250 g",
  "count": 24,
  "preset": "a4-24"
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/labels.pdf",
  "bytes": 2089,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 1,
  "labels": 24,
  "perSheet": 24,
  "shrunk": 0,
  "truncated": 0
}
```

<a id="doc_receipt"></a>
### `doc_receipt` — Receipt PDF

Make a simple sales receipt as PDF: an 80 mm thermal-roll receipt (page height fits the content, monospace) or an A5 page. Shop header (name, address, phone, tax number), receipt number with prefix, date/time, customer, items (name × qty × price), discount (% or fixed amount), tax % after discount, total, payment method, amount paid and change, note and footer. Same maths as the website: amounts rounded half-up to 0, 2 or 3 decimals. Also returns the totals and a 32-column plain-text version. English labels, Latin text only for now. Web version: https://s3eidat.github.io/receipt-maker/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `shop` | object | no (default `{"name":"","address":"","phone":"","taxId":"","currency":"JD","decimals":2,"prefix":"R-","footer":"Thank you!"}`) | Shop details: name, address, phone, taxId, currency, decimals, prefix, footer. |
| `no` | integer | no (default `1`) | Receipt number. |
| `when` | string | no | Date and time, YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM (default now). |
| `customer` | string | no (default `""`) | Customer name or table (optional). |
| `pay` | "cash" \| "card" \| "transfer" \| "wallet" \| "other" | no (default `"cash"`) | Payment method. |
| `items` | object[] | yes | Items: name, qty, price. |
| `disc` | number | no (default `0`) | Discount: a percentage, or an amount when discType is "amt". |
| `discType` | "pct" \| "amt" | no (default `"pct"`) | pct = percent of subtotal (default), amt = fixed amount. |
| `tax` | number | no (default `0`) | Tax rate % applied after the discount. |
| `paid` | number | no (default `0`) | Amount the customer paid (shows change). |
| `note` | string | no (default `""`) | Note line above the footer. |
| `format` | "thermal" \| "a5" | no (default `"thermal"`) | thermal = 80 mm roll (default), a5 = A5 page. |
| `output` | string | no | Where to write the result: a file path, or a folder ending in "/". Default: the server output folder (a temp folder unless --out-dir is set). |
| `returnBase64` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also return the output as base64 in the result (only for small files). Default false. |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | Absolute path of the written PDF. |
| `bytes` | number | Size in bytes. |
| `pages` | number | Number of pages. |
| `mimeType` | string |  |
| `base64` | string |  |
| `subtotal` | number |  |
| `discount` | number |  |
| `tax` | number |  |
| `total` | number |  |
| `paid` | number |  |
| `change` | number |  |
| `text` | string | Plain-text receipt (32 columns). |

**Example** — Subtotal 8.000, discount 0.800, tax 1.152, total 8.352 JD, change 1.648.

```json
{
  "shop": {
    "name": "Zaytoun Corner Café",
    "address": "Rainbow St. 12, Amman",
    "currency": "JD",
    "decimals": 3
  },
  "no": 1043,
  "customer": "Table 4",
  "paid": 10,
  "disc": 10,
  "tax": 16,
  "items": [
    {
      "name": "Cappuccino",
      "qty": 2,
      "price": 2.25
    },
    {
      "name": "Za'atar manakeesh",
      "qty": 1,
      "price": 1.5
    },
    {
      "name": "Fresh orange juice",
      "qty": 1,
      "price": 2
    }
  ]
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/receipt-R-1043.pdf",
  "bytes": 2400,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 1,
  "subtotal": 8,
  "discount": 0.8,
  "tax": 1.152,
  "total": 8.352,
  "paid": 10,
  "change": 1.648,
  "text": "      Zaytoun Corner Café\n     Rainbow St. 12, Amman\n--------------------------------\n            Receipt\nNo.                       R-1043\nDate           15/01/2026  09:30\nCustomer                 Table 4\n--------------------------------\nCappuccino\n  2 × 2.250 JD          4.500 JD\nZa'atar manakeesh\n  1 × 1.500 JD          1.500 JD\nFresh orange juice\n  1 × 2.000 JD          2.000 JD\n--------------------------------\nSubtotal                8.000 JD\nDiscount 10%           −0.800 JD\nTax 16%                 1.152 JD\nTOTAL                   8.352 JD\nPayment                     Cash\nPaid             …"
}
```

<a id="doc_resume"></a>
### `doc_resume` — Resume / CV PDF

Turn structured CV fields into a clean A4 resume PDF: contact header, summary, experience, education, skills, projects and languages, in any section order, with three templates like the website: "classic" (centred header and accent rule), "modern" (coloured sidebar with contact, skills and languages) and "compact" (denser, headings in a left column). Description lines starting with "-", "•", "*" or "–" become bullet points; skills are comma-separated. Aims for one page; longer CVs continue on a second page. English headings, Latin text only for now. Web version: https://s3eidat.github.io/resume-builder/

Hints: readOnly, idempotent

**Inputs**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `template` | "classic" \| "modern" \| "compact" | no (default `"classic"`) |  |
| `accent` | string | no (default `"#0e7c86"`) | Accent colour (hex). |
| `order` | ("summary" \| "experience" \| "education" \| "skills" \| "projects" \| "languages")[] | no | Section order; missing sections are appended in the default order. |
| `contact` | object | no (default `{"name":"","title":"","email":"","phone":"","location":"","website":""}`) |  |
| `summary` | string | no (default `""`) | Two or three lines about you. |
| `experience` | object[] | no (default `[]`) |  |
| `education` | object[] | no (default `[]`) |  |
| `skills` | string \| string[] | no (default `""`) | Skills: comma-separated text or a list. |
| `projects` | object[] | no (default `[]`) |  |
| `languages` | object[] | no (default `[]`) |  |
| `output` | string | no | Where to write the result: a file path, or a folder ending in "/". Default: the server output folder (a temp folder unless --out-dir is set). |
| `returnBase64` | boolean | no (default `false`) | Also return the output as base64 in the result (only for small files). Default false. |

**Output**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | Absolute path of the written PDF. |
| `bytes` | number | Size in bytes. |
| `pages` | number | Number of pages. |
| `mimeType` | string |  |
| `base64` | string |  |

**Example**

```json
{
  "template": "modern",
  "contact": {
    "name": "Layla Haddad",
    "title": "Senior Product Designer",
    "email": "layla@example.com",
    "location": "Amman, Jordan"
  },
  "summary": "Product designer with 8 years of experience.",
  "experience": [
    {
      "role": "Senior Product Designer",
      "org": "Mosaic Health",
      "start": "2022",
      "end": "Present",
      "desc": "- Redesigned patient booking; completion up 31%\n- Built a design system"
    }
  ],
  "skills": "Figma, Prototyping, User research",
  "languages": [
    {
      "name": "Arabic",
      "level": "Native"
    },
    {
      "name": "English",
      "level": "Fluent"
    }
  ]
}
```

Result:

```json
{
  "path": "/tmp/s3eidat-tools/Layla-Haddad.pdf",
  "bytes": 2360,
  "mimeType": "application/pdf",
  "pages": 1
}
```
